### Objective

Acceptance constants and comparison procedures are fixed independently of the
qualifying final result. Compact supervised rows use the independently reviewed
five-run predictive-learning contract, with unchanged historical floors and
explicit provenance; RL retains separately justified cohort bars. A lint bans
numeric thresholds derived from the value they grade. Persisted artifact binding is
implemented by Sprints `127.1`/`133.1`; this sprint owns the independent
external-truth predicates and exact served-byte proofs for its CPU/Apple rows
and both complete lane journals. Phase `279` owns the corresponding CUDA lane;
Phase `280` owns the three-lane aggregate and unchanged full CPU unit/integration
validation. The dependency sequence remains `278 → 279 → 280`.

### Deliverables

- `src/JitML/Product/ExternalBars.hs` verifies structural consistency against the
  unchanged canonical numeric tables; field names alone establish no external
  provenance. Compact SL acceptance additionally requires the versioned five-run
  learning procedure. Historical floors retain their actual origin and cannot
  stand in for that independent criterion. Dataset SHAs and arena baselines
  retain their canonical registries.
- A lint (`ProductTruth.hs`) statically rejects the `mkConvergenceBar … measuredValue 0.0`
  / `threshold = measured` pattern anywhere on a product path.
- The external-bar predicate re-derives `coPassed` from finite measurements
  rather than trusting a stored boolean. Current served-weight, manifest, blob,
  and dataset binding is not minted here; Sprint `133.1` exposes only an opaque
  admitted artifact for this harness to challenge.
- Bind each completed ProductRow claim to that opaque admitted manifest address
  and its exact served `supervised.weights` bytes, recompute the reported metric
  through the admitted runtime, and reject metadata-consistent byte
  substitution.
- Additional learning-signal predicates grade actual admitted serving outputs:
  classification beats every held-out constant and an exact uniform-binomial
  control; regression strictly beats the held-out mean. The supervised report
  contract versions this policy independently of metric-match tolerances.
- Compact qualification trains canonical base plus `0..4` in order, retains all
  five complete finite outcomes, requires at least three joint initialization
  improvements and reference-control successes, and grades the full five-run
  median against unchanged floors. Index `0` is independently eligible and
  remains the served artifact. Exact initial descriptors/bytes, final artifacts,
  actual counters, split/data/device identities and separate cohort statistics
  are replayable and authenticated by the producer/report/lane journal.
- Tiny ImageNet bounded training interleaves canonical class queues before
  splitting gradient and selection-validation prefixes. The selection contract
  participates in ProductRow `PlanId`; evaluation annotation order is preserved.
  These input repairs do not replace the independent learning qualification.
- Device TRPO Fisher products use fixed unit-direction central differences and
  host magnitude restoration to avoid tiny-direction Float32 collapse. The
  producer-owned numerical recipe versions all four TRPO PlanIds per lane;
  pure/real-device controls challenge curvature, scaling, and old-checkpoint
  completed admission. Full fixed-budget reward failures remain failures.
- On-policy final reward rollouts use the same selected `MlpDevice` as training,
  fail closed on invalid device output or execution failure, and retain the
  declared environment, masks, greedy tie rule, episode count, and horizon.
  A producer-owned evaluation recipe versions every affected ProductRow plan;
  old completed checkpoints cannot satisfy current publication by reuse.
- On-policy GAE distinguishes true termination from time-limit truncation.
  True termination has zero bootstrap; truncation uses the old policy/value
  parameters on the actual pre-reset successor, and both boundaries stop GAE
  recursion into the reset episode. Extra value forwards are not environment
  transitions. A separate rollout-recipe version binds the changed training
  semantics into the affected plans. Scalar and vectorized controls cover
  boundary precedence, reset leakage, and device bootstrap failures.
- Shared potential shaping uses zero successor potential at true termination.
  Continuations and time-limit truncations retain the actual pre-reset successor
  potential and bootstrap. DQN, QR-DQN and both PPO collectors use the environment
  termination flag; raw evaluation rewards and all qualification criteria remain
  unchanged. Fresh training is required after this correctness repair.
- Execution witnesses mint and hash the canonical physical artifact coordinate,
  retaining strict equality of all six witness fields. Separate real processes
  challenge shared-cache identity across working directories and independent-cache
  distinction. Bounded refusal diagnostics retain actual typed child findings and
  finer parent-evaluation detail without changing acceptance or failure classes.
